# Smart Chat Bar Capability Checklist

Last updated: 2026-07-13

Scope: Windows desktop smart chat bar capabilities across natural-language entry, slash commands, context injection, execution control, runtime modes, multi-agent work, memory/skills/workflows, and cross-channel chat.

Legend: `[x]` complete and verified, `[~]` partial with known gaps, `[ ]` not implemented.

## Current Run Addendum 2026-07-13 JS Bundler Config Input

Agent `js-bundler-config-input-agent` design: extend the existing JS/TS tooling config channel for reviewed workspace-local `vite.config.*`, `rollup.config.*`, and `tsup.config.*` build/bundler configs without running Node, npm, pnpm, Yarn, Bun, Vite dev servers, Rollup/Tsup builds, lint/test/format runners, config module imports, environment loading, plugin resolution, networks, credential lookup, workspace mutation, or provider sends. `channelAdapters.ts` now normalizes Vite/Rollup/Tsup configs to `.js-tooling-config`, labels them as Vite, Rollup, or Tsup, surfaces bounded `plugins`, `server`, `preview`, `build`, `rollupOptions`, `input`, `output`, `external`, `entry`, `format`, `target`, `dts`, `outDir`, `sourcemap`, and `minify` cues through the redacted JS/TS tooling preview, and preserves `application/vnd.drsai.js-tooling-config` MIME provenance. Runtime `vite.config.ts`, `rollup.config.mjs`, and `tsup.config.ts` golden fixtures validate bundler metadata, build/server/output evidence, secret redaction, MIME provenance, and no-dev-server/no-build safety copy. This improves `[~]` Voice, image, and file input and `[~]` Voice, image, and file channel inputs for local JavaScript build handoffs; live dev-server validation, real bundler execution, plugin resolution, dependency graph analysis, bundle diagnostics, and provider-backed project analysis remain `[~]`. `npm run verify:channel-adapters`, `npm run verify:channel-adapter-route-order`, `npm run verify:channel-adapter-runtime-fixtures`, `npm run typecheck:node`, and `npm run verify:chatbar-checklist` cover this slice.

## Current Run Addendum 2026-07-13 Gemini Error Taxonomy

Agent `gemini-error-taxonomy-agent` design: continue `provider-runtime-parity-agent` work by normalizing Google/Gemini-style structured error frames locally without changing live provider transport, backend dispatch, model selection, credentials, tool execution, persisted transcripts, renderer layout, connector state, or network behavior. `sseParser.ts` now recognizes Gemini error payloads with Google `error.status` / `error.details[].@type` evidence even when they arrive on generic `event: error` frames, maps them to provider `google_gemini` instead of Anthropic, uses `error.status` such as `RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED` as the canonical analytics code, and marks `RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED`, `UNAVAILABLE`, `DEADLINE_EXCEEDED`, and `ABORTED` as retryable Gemini categories. `parseChatSseFrame` throws `ChatSseError` with that normalized code/retryable flag, while `parseProviderErrorAnalyticsSseFrame` emits `Gemini stream error.` summaries without persisting raw provider payloads. `verify-chat-sse-parser.mjs` adds Gemini quota-error throw and analytics fixtures; `verify-provider-error-analytics.mjs` persists a `google_gemini` provider error record and checks raw-payload non-leakage. This improves `[~]` Diff/log/tool-call visibility and provider observability; live Gemini transport validation, backend provider parity, persisted cross-run timeline search, and unified backend plan/tool events remain `[~]`. `npm run verify:chat-sse`, `npm run verify:provider-error-analytics`, `npm run typecheck:node`, and `npm run verify:chatbar-checklist` cover this slice.

## Current Run Addendum 2026-07-10 Clipboard Text/Image Paste Context

The composer now handles explicit textarea paste events for clipboard images while preserving any accompanying pasted text at the cursor. Pasted image clipboard files are attached as reviewed `selection` context chips with non-filesystem `clipboard:image:*` paths, bounded by `MAX_CLIPBOARD_IMAGE_COUNT` and `MAX_CLIPBOARD_IMAGE_BYTES`; oversized images degrade to metadata-only visible context instead of sending a data URL. The attachment copy makes the boundary explicit: no OCR, vision model, filesystem write, network call, or provider send is performed while preparing clipboard context. `npm run verify:clipboard-context`, `npm run typecheck:web`, and `npm run verify:chatbar-checklist` cover this Clipboard Text/Image Paste Context slice; OS-level clipboard polling, clipboard file-path imports, OCR/vision extraction, image editing, and mobile clipboard handoff remain `[~]`.

## Current Run Addendum 2026-07-10 Clipboard Local Path Paste Mentions

The composer now treats explicit pasted local path text as reviewed inline context references. When a paste event contains absolute Windows/local paths and no clipboard image payload, `normalizePastedLocalPathMentions` rewrites up to `MAX_CLIPBOARD_PATH_MENTIONS` unique paths into visible `@file:"..."` / `@folder:"..."` mentions so the existing inline context assembler can create reviewable chips before send. The paste handler does not poll the OS clipboard, read files or folders, attach hidden file contents, call the network, or send anything to a provider while preparing these mentions. `npm run verify:clipboard-context`, `npm run typecheck:web`, and `npm run verify:chatbar-checklist` cover this Clipboard Local Path Paste Mentions slice; OS-level clipboard monitoring, shell file-drop metadata, existence/type validation, file content previews from paste, and mobile clipboard handoff remain `[~]`.

## Current Run Addendum 2026-07-11 Queued @folder Summary Context

The composer now normalizes all queued `folder` attachments through `summarizeQueuedContextAttachments` before `onSubmit`, not only folders added through the picker or inline mention path. The summary provider uses the injected `onSummarizeWorkspaceFolder` callback when present and safely falls back to `desktopApi.summarizeWorkspaceFolder` only when the desktop bridge is available. Each folder remains a visible reviewed context chip with `visibleText: summary.summary`, bounded file/folder counts, estimated tokens, and failure notes when summary generation is unavailable. At this boundary, the renderer does not silently attach full directory contents, recurse outside the reviewed folder summary contract, call providers, or send hidden files. `npm run verify:context-assembler`, `npm run typecheck:web`, `npm run typecheck:node`, and `npm run verify:chatbar-checklist` cover this slice; provider-exact tokenizer accounting and packaged IDE folder-context smoke remain `[~]`.

## Verification Matrix

| Area | Automated verification | Manual verification |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Checklist coverage | `npm run verify:chatbar-checklist` | Read this file when adding or closing feature points. |
| Provider telemetry | `npm run verify:chat-sse`, `npm run verify:provider-usage-analytics` | Inspect `DRSAI_HOME\desktop\provider-usage-analytics.json` after a streamed provider response with token usage. |
| Slash/runtime modes | `npm run verify:chat-commands`, `npm run verify:runtime-mode` | Type each slash command and confirm visible composer state. |
| Context injection | `npm run verify:context-assembler`, `npm run verify:workspace-context`, `npm run verify:ide-context` | Attach file/folder/selection/IDE context and inspect chips before send. |
| Execution control | `npm run verify:execution-policy`, `npm run verify:approval-center` | Queue shell/git/MCP/connector approvals and approve/reject/cancel them. |
| Multi-agent/fork | `npm run verify:fork-worktree`, `npm run verify:thread-menu`, `npm run verify:e2e-fork-merge` after `npm run build:unpack` | Create fork queue, dispatch, inspect thread menu, review merge-back, and run packaged approval smoke when a build exists. |
| Memory/workflows | `npm run verify:project-memory`, `npm run verify:workflow-marketplace`, `npm run verify:background-tasks`, `npm run verify:scheduled-tasks` | Add retrospective memory, generate a skill draft, and start a workflow recipe. |
| Packaged smoke | `npm run verify:bundled-backend-source`, `npm run verify:packaged` | Confirm the generated backend source manifest/payload is present before running real packaged IPC smoke. |
| Cross-channel | `npm run verify:channel-adapters`, `npm run verify:channel-adapter-route-order`, `npm run verify:channel-adapter-runtime-fixtures` | Import workspace-local `.drsai/*-context.json` snapshots, route to chat, inspect outbound delivery ledger, review ambiguous file route precedence, and run temporary workspace fixtures through `importChannelContext`. |

## Current Run Addendum 2026-07-09 Channel Adapter Route-Order Verification

Channels `file-input` verification now includes `npm run verify:channel-adapter-route-order`, a static route precedence verifier for representative ambiguous fixtures such as coverage/test-report XML, SARIF/package JSON, Docker Compose YAML, stylesheet, patch/diff, package-manager config, Python dependency, Go module, scientific container, and generic config fallbacks. The verifier reads `src/main/channelAdapters.ts` and confirms specialized routes stay ahead of generic document, JSON/YAML, source-code, binary, config/log, and raw-text fallbacks while also checking normalized extension markers such as `CMakeLists.txt`, `compile_commands.json`, `gradle.properties`, `.mvn/maven.config`, and `.mvn/jvm.config`. This improves test coverage for the expanding static import router without launching providers, runtimes, package managers, compilers, or external tools. Runtime route golden fixtures now cover representative selected-file output through `importChannelContext`; remaining `[~]` work is packaged Electron IPC fixture execution and exhaustive golden coverage for the full importer matrix.

## Current Run Addendum 2026-07-07 External Runtime Reconnect

`external_runtime` workflow steps now exist in the shared workflow contract, marketplace recipe catalog, main workflow run engine, mock bridge, and Skills Square restart UI. After app restart, these steps become explicit `reconnect_external` resume actions with copy that says no provider process is auto-started. This partially closes the previous `[ ]` autonomous runtime reconnect item by adding a safe restart recovery contract; real provider-owned process supervision and automatic reconnect are still `[~]` follow-up work. `npm run verify:workflow-marketplace`, `npm run verify:background-tasks`, `npm run verify:scheduled-tasks`, and TypeScript checks cover this slice.

## Current Run Addendum 2026-07-08 Script File Inputs

Channels `file-input` now handles reviewed workspace-local script files through bounded source-code static previews, including `.ps1`, `.psm1`, `.psd1`, `.bat`, `.cmd`, `.sh`, `.bash`, and `.zsh`. The preview extracts module/source/call hints, functions or batch labels, local static review cues, process-boundary hints, and MIME provenance, while preserving the existing no-execution boundary: no PowerShell/cmd/bash process is launched, no build/test/shell command is run, no dependency installation occurs, and no provider/network call is made. This advances voice/image/file channel input coverage; live script execution, shell linting, policy signing, dependency resolution, and sandboxed dry-run remain `[~]`. `npm run verify:channel-adapters`, `npm run verify:chatbar-checklist`, and `npm run typecheck:node` cover this slice.

## Current Run Addendum 2026-07-07 Local Marketplace Sync

Skills Square now has a reviewed local marketplace slice: project skill drafts can be published into a desktop-data `skill-marketplace-submissions/<workspace>/<slug>/submission.json` plus `SKILL.md` review package, and Workflow marketplace can sync bounded templates from workspace-local `.drsai/workflow-marketplace.json` into the existing prepare/run path. This moves curated publishing/live marketplace sync from `[ ]` to `[~]` without starting OAuth, remote publishing, network marketplace sync, or automatic third-party execution. `npm run verify:workflow-marketplace`, `npm run typecheck:node`, and `npm run typecheck:web` cover this slice.

## Current Run Addendum 2026-07-07 Dependency Test Graph

Fork merge conflict review now augments the existing path-rule test graph with content-derived dependency hints. The renderer scans conflict preview content for `import`, `export ... from`, `require()`, dynamic `import()`, and changed `package.json` test/typecheck/verify/build scripts, resolves relative/aliased local imports into repository path candidates with extension and `index.*` expansion, then shows `Repository dependency graph`, `Import dependency graph`, and `Package script graph` suggestions beside the repo path matches before users stage a resolved draft. This partially advances generated fork merge test graph work; a full reverse dependency index and compiler-backed AST diffing remain `[~]`. `npm run verify:fork-worktree` and `npm run typecheck:web` cover this slice.

## Current Run Addendum 2026-07-07 Document Input Summaries

Channels `file-input` now attempts bounded read-only document text previews for workspace-local `.docx` files and simple PDF text operators before falling back to explicit attachment review copy. Extraction is size-capped, local-only, uses no external programs or provider calls, and keeps routing into chat behind the existing visible Attach/Route action. This partially advances voice/image/file input quality; live microphone capture, transcription, OCR, and full PDF layout extraction remain `[~]`. `npm run verify:channel-adapters`, `npm run typecheck:node`, and `npm run verify:chatbar-checklist` cover this slice.

## Current Run Addendum 2026-07-07 Connector Auth Preparation

Channels now exposes `Prepare auth` for mobile, Slack, GitHub, Docs, and Calendar adapters. The new `desktop:channel-adapter-auth-start` path creates bounded workspace-scoped authorization preparation metadata with provider scopes, a short user code, verification URI, expiry, `authPreparedAt`, and placeholder credential state, but does not open a browser, poll providers, store tokens, or enable live sends. This advances connector/mobile handshakes from passive session stubs to a reviewed local preparation contract; real polling, token exchange, credential storage, live provider sync, and real sends remain `[~]`. `npm run verify:channel-adapters`, `npm run typecheck:node`, and `npm run typecheck:web` cover this slice.

## Current Run Addendum 2026-07-07 Reverse Dependency Test Graph

Fork conflict review now includes a maintained local reverse dependency index in addition to path, import, repository-candidate, and package-script graph suggestions. When conflicts touch shared API contracts, main IPC, preload/mock bridge code, Channels, MCP, fork runtime, workflow runtime, or generic main/shared/renderer modules, the workbench can recommend verifier and typecheck commands for known downstream consumers before staging a resolved draft. This advances reverse dependency coverage without scanning the full repository or requiring compiler services; full repository-wide import indexing and compiler-backed AST diffing remain `[~]`. `npm run verify:fork-worktree` and `npm run typecheck:web` cover this slice.

## Current Run Addendum 2026-07-07 Model-Aware Context Budget

The chat composer now derives its visible context budget from the selected model metadata when `token_limit` or `max_tokens` is available, reserves 2048 tokens for output, clamps tiny model budgets to a minimum safe preview budget, and falls back to the previous 12000-token recommendation when no model limit is known. The context preview pill shows whether the estimate came from a model limit or fallback budget, and `npm run verify:context-assembler` asserts the model-aware contract. This advances backend/model-limit alignment; exact tokenizer parity with the backend remains `[~]`.

## Current Run Addendum 2026-07-07 Export Surface Test Graph

Fork conflict review now detects changed public export surfaces in TypeScript/JavaScript modules and Python `__all__` lists, then adds `Export surface graph` suggestions to the same repo test graph shown before draft write-back or staging. Shared API changes recommend node/web typechecks and approval/chat verifiers, renderer exports recommend web typecheck/UI verification, and main/runtime exports recommend node typecheck plus feature verifiers where the path is recognizable. This narrows merge-back review risk without filesystem scans or provider calls; full repository-wide import indexing and compiler-backed AST analysis remain `[~]`. `npm run verify:fork-worktree` and `npm run typecheck:web` cover this slice.

## Current Run Addendum 2026-07-07 Repository Import Index

Fork conflict review now includes a maintained `FORK_CONFLICT_REPOSITORY_IMPORT_INDEX` beside the existing path, import, reverse, package-script, and export-surface suggestions. The analyzer normalizes changed module paths and local import candidates into module keys, then emits `Repository import index` suggestions that name downstream importer surfaces and commands for shared API, execution policy, channel adapters, MCP runtime, fork conflict analysis, workflow runtime, and workspace checkpoints. This advances full import-index coverage without filesystem scans or compiler services; generated source-derived import-index extraction remains `[~]`. `npm run verify:fork-worktree` and `npm run typecheck:web` cover this slice.

## Current Run Addendum 2026-07-07 Generated Repository Import Index

Fork conflict review now includes a source-derived static import index generated from the Windows app's local TypeScript/JavaScript import metadata. `npm run generate:fork-import-index` scans `src/main`, `src/preload`, `src/renderer/src`, and `src/shared`, resolves relative plus `@shared` / `@renderer` imports, writes `forkConflictGeneratedImportIndex.ts`, and the conflict analyzer emits `Generated repository import index` suggestions before maintained fallback rules. This moves import-index generation from a future gap to a partial capability; compiler AST diffing and broader language import extraction remain `[~]`. `npm run verify:fork-worktree` and `npm run typecheck:web` cover this slice.

## Current Run Addendum 2026-07-07 Workflow Retrospective Prompt

Skills Square now watches the current workspace's latest workflow run and, when it reaches `complete` or `blocked`, displays an editable `Workflow retrospective candidate` draft. Saving the prompt writes reviewed `source: "retrospective"` project memory through the existing local API; dismissing it writes nothing. This closes the automatic post-task review prompt gap while keeping skill draft creation, local install, and marketplace publication behind explicit human review. `npm run verify:project-memory`, `npm run verify:workflow-marketplace`, and `npm run typecheck:web` cover this slice.

## Current Run Addendum 2026-07-08 Import-Kind Provenance

Fork conflict review generated import-index suggestions now retain source-derived edge provenance. `scripts/generate-fork-import-index.mjs` classifies each local dependency edge as `import`, `re-export`, `require`, or `dynamic-import`, writes that `kind` into `forkConflictGeneratedImportIndex.ts`, and the semantic preview labels downstream suggestions with `imports`, `re-exports`, `requires`, or `dynamically imports`. This improves merge-back test selection for barrel modules and public API surfaces without adding runtime filesystem scans or compiler services. `npm run generate:fork-import-index`, `npm run verify:fork-worktree`, and `npm run typecheck:web` cover this slice.

## Current Run Addendum 2026-07-08 Python Import Dependency Graph

Fork conflict review now derives dependency graph suggestions from Python imports in conflict previews. The analyzer parses `import` and `from ... import ...`, maps relative imports plus `drsai`, `drsai_ext`, and `drsai_ui` packages to repository path candidates, accounts for `__init__.py` package modules, and labels local Python dependency suggestions before resolved drafts are staged. This reduces Python merge-back blind spots without adding runtime filesystem scans, provider calls, or compiler services. `npm run verify:fork-worktree` and `npm run typecheck:web` cover this slice.

## Current Run Addendum 2026-07-08 Compiler AST Export Index

Fork conflict review now has a source-derived TypeScript compiler AST export index. `scripts/generate-fork-import-index.mjs` uses the local TypeScript compiler API to parse TypeScript/JavaScript source files, emits `FORK_CONFLICT_GENERATED_AST_EXPORT_INDEX` with exported symbol names and compiler syntax kinds, and the conflict analyzer adds `Compiler AST export graph` suggestions when changed public symbols match that generated module surface. Fork conflict review now parses live TypeScript/JavaScript conflict preview content for export syntax deltas across base, source, fork, and resolved draft. This improves live merge-back review without bundling the TypeScript compiler into the renderer; packaged merge-back smoke and richer compiler-service diagnostics remain `[~]`. `npm run verify:fork-worktree`, `npm run verify:chatbar-checklist`, and `npm run typecheck:web` cover this slice.

## Current Run Addendum 2026-07-08 Compiler Diagnostic Coverage

Fork conflict review now has a generated TypeScript compiler diagnostic coverage index beside the AST export index. `scripts/generate-fork-import-index.mjs` records each TS/JS module's script kind, current compiler syntax diagnostic hints, and targeted verification commands, while the conflict analyzer adds `Compiler diagnostics graph` suggestions for matching conflict modules. This improves compiler-backed merge guidance without bundling compiler services into the renderer or executing live toolchains during review; fully live diagnostics for edited draft content remain `[~]`. `npm run generate:fork-import-index`, `npm run verify:fork-worktree`, `npm run verify:chatbar-checklist`, and `npm run typecheck:web` cover this slice.

## Current Run Addendum 2026-07-08 Live Draft Diagnostic Preflight

Fork conflict review now inspects the edited resolved draft for compiler-blocking syntax before write-back or staging. The browser-safe preflight flags remaining conflict markers, unmatched or unclosed delimiters, unterminated strings/templates, and unterminated block comments, then adds `Live draft compiler diagnostic preflight` test graph suggestions before the static generated compiler diagnostic coverage hints. This advances live draft diagnostics without loading TypeScript compiler services into the renderer; full project compiler-service semantic diagnostics for edited drafts remain `[~]`. `npm run verify:fork-worktree`, `npm run verify:chatbar-checklist`, and `npm run typecheck:web` cover this slice.

## Current Run Addendum 2026-07-08 Backend-Aligned Context Token Estimate

The chat composer now estimates visible context from a backend-shaped prompt preview for attachments and workspace instructions, including attachment headers, kind/path/content labels, browser/terminal/selection/folder body labels, a fixed system-preamble overhead, and a mixed ASCII/CJK/punctuation heuristic instead of plain character-count division. This narrows the remaining context-budget gap without adding provider tokenizer dependencies or changing the actual send path. Provider-exact tokenizer parity for configured models remains `[~]`. `npm run verify:context-assembler`, `npm run verify:chatbar-checklist`, and `npm run typecheck:web` cover this slice.

## Current Run Addendum 2026-07-08 Tokenizer Calibration Samples

Model catalog entries can now include bounded `tokenizer_calibration` samples with known provider token counts. The main process normalizes and caps those samples, the composer derives a median calibration factor from the selected model before computing the visible context budget, and the context budget tooltip shows raw versus calibrated estimates plus the calibration sample count. This advances provider-exact token accounting without network calls or tokenizer dependencies; real provider sample packs for each configured model remain `[~]`. `npm run verify:context-assembler`, `npm run verify:chatbar-checklist`, and `npm run typecheck:web` cover this slice.

## Current Run Addendum 2026-07-08 Packaged Fork Merge-Back Approval Smoke

Fork merge-back review now has a packaged Electron smoke path that exercises real preload IPC approval gating, approved merge execution, approved cleanup, and conflict recovery against throwaway Git fixtures. `OPENDRSAI_E2E_FORK_MERGE` first creates temporary source/worktree workspaces, queues `requestForkLifecycleApproval({ action: "merge_back" })`, rejects it through `decideApproval`, and confirms the fork lifecycle stays `active`. It then creates a temporary Git repo plus real worktree under the smoke `DRSAI_HOME`, registers both paths as trusted workspaces, queues and approves merge-back through the same Approval Center API, verifies source content, advanced HEAD, two-parent merge commit, and `merged` metadata, then queues and approves discard cleanup and verifies closed lifecycle state, removed worktree, and merged branch deletion. A separate conflict fixture verifies approved merge-back moves to `merge_pending`, preserves the source HEAD/content, and aborts the failed Git merge cleanly. `npm run verify:fork-worktree` covers the source contract, `npm run typecheck:node` covers the E2E hook, and `npm run verify:e2e-fork-merge` runs the real packaged app after `npm run build:unpack`.

## Current Run Addendum 2026-07-08 Generated Python Import Index

Fork conflict review now feeds Python source imports into the generated repository import index, not only live conflict-preview heuristics. `scripts/generate-fork-import-index.mjs` scans the local `drsai`, `drsai_ext`, and `drsai_ui` Python package roots, extracts `import` / `from ... import ...` edges, resolves relative package imports plus `__init__.py` module keys, emits `python-import` provenance, and the conflict analyzer labels generated downstream suggestions as Python imports before merge-back staging. This reduces Python module conflict blind spots without runtime filesystem scans, provider calls, or compiler services. `npm run generate:fork-import-index`, `npm run verify:fork-worktree`, `npm run verify:chatbar-checklist`, and `npm run typecheck:web` cover this slice.

## Current Run Addendum 2026-07-08 Workspace Tokenizer Calibration Packs

The model catalog now merges reviewed workspace-local tokenizer sample packs from `.drsai/tokenizer-calibration.json` into gateway-provided `tokenizer_calibration` metadata. The main process only reads the file through the allowed workspace IPC path, matches samples by model alias, provider model name, or display name, reuses the existing sample count and sample text caps, and never calls providers or tokenizer libraries. This advances provider-exact context budgeting from static config-only samples to per-workspace sample packs; collecting real provider-measured sample packs and comparing drift over time remain `[~]`. `npm run verify:context-assembler`, `npm run typecheck:node`, and `npm run typecheck:web` cover this slice.

## Current Run Addendum 2026-07-08 Tokenizer Calibration Drift Signal

The context budget meter now turns tokenizer calibration samples into an auditable drift signal. The renderer still uses the median provider/local-token ratio for the calibrated estimate, but it also calculates sample ratio spread, labels single-sample, low, medium, or high calibration drift, and shows that signal in the context budget tooltip and meter before send. This improves reviewed sample-pack quality checks without network calls or tokenizer dependencies; collecting real provider-measured samples for configured models remains `[~]`. `npm run verify:context-assembler`, `npm run verify:chatbar-checklist`, and `npm run typecheck:web` cover this slice.

## Current Run Addendum 2026-07-08 Workspace-Local Outbound Outbox

Approved outbound connector drafts can now complete a safe local runtime path when the workspace explicitly provides `.drsai/channel-outbox.json`. After Approval Center approval, the runtime validates the config is a regular workspace file, keeps the output JSONL inside the workspace, respects optional `allowedAdapters`, appends the reviewed draft to `.drsai/channel-outbox-deliveries.jsonl` by default, and records `runtime: "workspace_local_outbox"` plus `outboxPath` in the durable delivery ledger. Without that config, approved drafts keep the prior blocked missing-live-provider result. This advances actual connector send execution one local, reviewable step without OAuth, provider APIs, or network sends; real provider sends and token exchange remain `[~]`. `npm run verify:channel-adapters`, `npm run typecheck:node`, and `npm run typecheck:web` cover this slice.

## Current Run Addendum 2026-07-08 EML Message File Input
